const VIEW_GROUPS={
  painel:{views:['painel']},
  pubs:{views:['pubs']},
  pessoas:{views:['rede','track'],labels:{rede:'Rede',track:'Acompanhamento'}},
  agenda:{views:['crono']},
  conversas:{views:['mensagens','conversas'],labels:{mensagens:'Fofoca geral',conversas:'Privadas'},badges:{mensagens:'msgBadge',conversas:'privateBadge'}},
  mural:{views:['mural','fotos','destaques'],labels:{mural:'Mural',fotos:'Fotos',destaques:'Destaques'},badges:{mural:'muralBadge'}},
  lab:{views:['lab']},
  medalhas:{views:['medalhas']},
  jogo:{views:['jogo']}
};
function groupOfView(v){return Object.keys(VIEW_GROUPS).find(g=>VIEW_GROUPS[g].views.includes(v))||v}
function renderSubNav(v){
  const nav=$('#subNav');if(!nav)return;
  const g=VIEW_GROUPS[groupOfView(v)];
  if(!g||g.views.length<2){nav.style.display='none';nav.innerHTML='';return}
  nav.style.display='flex';
  nav.innerHTML=g.views.map(x=>{const b=g.badges?.[x],bd=b?$('#'+b):null;const n=bd&&bd.style.display!=='none'?`<span class="badge">${esc(bd.textContent)}</span>`:'';return `<button type="button" class="${x===v?'on':''}" data-sub-view="${x}">${esc(g.labels[x])}${n}</button>`}).join('');
  /* P203H1: Panelinhas é uma aba real da navegação. Antes ela dependia de um
     patch posterior recriar o botão; como este script é type=module, aquela
     ponte podia desaparecer toda vez que renderSubNav reconstruía o HTML. */
  if(groupOfView(v)==='conversas'){
    const priv=nav.querySelector('[data-sub-view="conversas"]');
    if(priv&&!nav.querySelector('.p46-panel-btn')){
      const pan=document.createElement('button');
      pan.type='button';
      pan.className='p46-panel-btn';
      pan.textContent='Conversas em panelinha';
      pan.setAttribute('aria-label','Conversas em panelinha');
      pan.onclick=()=>{ if(typeof window.openPanelinhas==='function') window.openPanelinhas(); };
      nav.insertBefore(pan,priv);
    }
  }
  nav.querySelectorAll('[data-sub-view]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>switchView(b.dataset.subView));
}
function switchView(v){
  currentView=v;
  document.body.dataset.view=v;
  const grp=groupOfView(v);
  $$('.view').forEach(el=>el.classList.remove('on'));
  $$('.tab').forEach(el=>el.classList.toggle('on',(el.dataset.group||el.dataset.view)===grp));
  renderSubNav(v);
  const map={painel:'viewPainel',rede:'viewRede',crono:'viewCrono',track:'viewTrack',lab:'viewLab',feed:'viewFeed',mensagens:'viewMensagens',conversas:'viewConversas',mural:'viewMural',fotos:'viewFotos',destaques:'viewDestaques',pubs:'viewPubs',medalhas:'viewMedalhas',jogo:'viewJogo'};
  map[v] && $('#'+map[v]).classList.add('on');
  if(v==='painel') renderPainel();
  if(v==='rede') renderAll();
  if(v==='crono') renderCrono();
  if(v==='track') renderTrack();
  if(v==='lab'){ renderLab(); checkScienceEngine(); }
  if(v==='feed') renderFeed();
  if(v==='mensagens'){renderMensagens();markQuickSeen();}
  if(v==='conversas'){renderPrivateThreads();updatePrivateBadge();const t=state.privateThreads.find(x=>x.id===privateActiveThreadId);if(t)markPrivateThreadRead(t).catch(()=>{});}
  if(v==='mural'){ renderMural(); markChatSeen(); }
  if(v==='fotos') renderFotos();
  if(v==='pubs'){ renderPubs(); checkScienceEngine(); }
  if(v==='medalhas') renderMedals();
  $('#side').classList.remove('open');
  if($('#appMenuBackdrop')){ $('#appMenuBackdrop').classList.remove('open'); $('#appMenuBackdrop').setAttribute('aria-hidden','true'); }
}

/* ============ RENDER ALL (rede) ============ */
function renderAll(){
  renderStats(); renderLegend(); renderLinhaFilters(); renderProgramaFilter(); renderGraph();
}
function clearFilters(){
  hiddenNiveis.clear(); hiddenLinhas.clear();
  $('#search').value=''; $('#fPrograma').value=''; $('#fStatus').value='';
  renderAll();
}

 <aside class="p46-side"><div class="p46-side-head"><b id="p46SideTitle">Fofoca geral</b><small id="p46SideSub">Assuntos e panelinhas da equipe</small></div><input class="p46-search" id="p46Search" placeholder="Buscar conversa ou tema…"><div class="p46-side-list" id="p46SideList"></div><button class="p46-newgroup" id="p46NewGroup">＋ Nova panelinha</button></aside>
 <section class="p46-main"><div class="p46-chat-head"><div class="p46-chat-icon" id="p46ChatIcon">💬</div><div class="p46-chat-title"><b id="p46ChatTitle">Fofoca geral</b><small id="p46ChatSub">Conversa aberta da equipe</small></div><button class="p46-mobile-groups" id="p46MobileGroups">☰ Conversas</button><div class="p46-head-actions"><button id="p46MuralBtn" title="Ir para o mural">📣 Mural</button></div></div><div class="p46-topics" id="p46Topics"></div><div class="p46-msgs" id="p46Msgs"></div><div class="p46-compose"><div class="p46-replying" id="p46Replying"><div class="rtext" id="p46ReplyText"></div><button id="p46CancelReply">×</button></div><div class="p46-compose-row"><select class="p46-topic-select" id="p46TopicSelect"></select><textarea class="p46-input" id="p46Input" rows="1" placeholder="Mensagem…"></textarea><button class="p46-send" id="p46Send" title="Enviar">➤</button></div></div></section>
 </div>`}

function ensureMount(){const view=$('#viewMensagens');if(!view)return false;if(!view.querySelector('.p46-shell')){view.innerHTML=viewHTML();bindUI();mounted=true}return true}

function bindUI(){
 $('#p46Input')?.addEventListener('input',e=>{e.target.style.height='auto';e.target.style.height=Math.min(120,e.target.scrollHeight)+'px'});
 $('#p46Input')?.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{if(e.key==='Enter'&&!e.shiftKey){e.preventDefault();sendMessage()}});
 $('#p46Send').onclick=sendMessage;
 $('#p46CancelReply').onclick=()=>{replyTarget=null;renderReply()};
 $('#p46MuralBtn').onclick=()=>{if(typeof window.switchView==='function')window.switchView('mural')};
 $('#p46NewGroup').onclick=openGroupModal;
 $('#p46Search').oninput=renderSide;
 $('#p46TopicSelect').onchange=e=>{if(e.target.value==='__new__'){const v=prompt('Nome do novo tema:');if(norm(v)){composerTopic=norm(v).slice(0,40)}renderTopics()}else composerTopic=e.target.value};
 $('#p46MobileGroups').onclick=()=>{const side=$('.p46-side');if(!side)return;side.style.display=side.style.display==='flex'?'none':'flex';side.style.position='absolute';side.style.zIndex='80';side.style.inset='0 12% 0 0';side.style.boxShadow='0 20px 60px rgba(0,0,0,.3)'};
}

function renderSide(){if(!ensureMount())return;const box=$('#p46SideList'),q=norm($('#p46Search')?.value).toLowerCase();if(!box)return;let html='';
 html+=`<div class="p46-side-title">Geral</div><button class="p46-list-btn ${mode==='general'?'on':''}" data-p46-general><span class="ico">💬</span><span class="txt"><b>Fofoca geral</b><small>toda a equipe · filtre por tema</small></span></button>`;
 const groups=groupMetas().filter(g=>!q||norm(g.groupName).toLowerCase().includes(q));html+=`<div class="p46-side-title">Conversas em panelinha</div>`;
 if(!groups.length)html+='<div style="padding:10px;color:#819198;font-size:11px">Nenhuma panelinha sua ainda.</div>';
 groups.forEach(g=>{const lm=groupMessages(g.groupId).slice(-1)[0],count=(g.memberIds||[]).length;html+=`<button class="p46-list-btn ${mode==='groups'&&activeGroupId===g.groupId?'on':''}" data-p46-group="${esc(g.groupId)}"><span class="ico">👥</span><span class="txt"><b>${esc(g.groupName||'Panelinha')}</b><small>${lm?esc((lm.text||'').slice(0,55)):`${count} participantes`}</small></span></button>`});box.innerHTML=html;
 box.querySelector('[data-p46-general]')?.addEventListener('click',()=>setMode('general'));
 box.querySelectorAll('[data-p46-group]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{activeGroupId=b.dataset.p46Group||'';setMode('groups')});

function ensureGroupModal(){let ov=$('#p46GroupOverlay');if(ov)return ov;ov=document.createElement('div');ov.id='p46GroupOverlay';ov.innerHTML=`<div class="p46-modal"><div class="p46-modal-head"><b>Nova conversa em panelinha</b><button type="button" id="p46GroupClose">×</button></div><div class="p46-modal-body"><input type="text" id="p46GroupName" placeholder="Nome da panelinha — ex.: Artigo do Pantanal"><input type="text" id="p46PersonSearch" placeholder="Buscar participante…"><div class="p46-people" id="p46People"></div><p style="font-size:10.5px;color:#75888f;line-height:1.4">A panelinha organiza conversas de um grupo específico. Para conversa estritamente privada entre duas pessoas, continue usando <b>Privadas</b>.</p></div><div class="p46-modal-foot"><button id="p46GroupCancel">Cancelar</button><button class="primary" id="p46GroupSave">Criar panelinha</button></div></div>`;document.body.appendChild(ov);$('#p46GroupClose').onclick=$('#p46GroupCancel').onclick=()=>ov.style.display='none';$('#p46PersonSearch').oninput=renderPeople;$('#p46GroupSave').onclick=createGroup;ov.addEventListener('click',e=>{if(e.target===ov)ov.style.display='none'});return ov}
function renderPeople(){const box=$('#p46People');if(!box)return;const q=norm($('#p46PersonSearch')?.value).toLowerCase();box.innerHTML=members.filter(m=>m.status!=='inativo'&&(!q||norm(m.nome).toLowerCase().includes(q))).sort((a,b)=>norm(a.nome).localeCompare(norm(b.nome),'pt-BR')).map(m=>`<label class="p46-person"><input type="checkbox" value="${esc(m.id)}" ${m.id===myMemberId?'checked disabled':''}><span>${esc(m.nome||'Carbonauta')}</span><small>${esc(m.nivel||'')}</small></label>`).join('')}

async function createGroup(){const name=norm($('#p46GroupName')?.value);if(!name)return toast('Dê um nome para a panelinha.');const ids=Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('#p46People input:checked')).map(x=>x.value);if(!ids.includes(myMemberId))ids.push(myMemberId);if(ids.length<2)return toast('Escolha pelo menos mais uma pessoa.');const gid='g_'+Date.now().toString(36)+'_'+Math.random().toString(36).slice(2,7),m=member(myMemberId);try{await f().addDoc(f().collection(window.db,'rede_chat'),{tipo:'chat_group_meta',groupId:gid,groupName:name.slice(0,80),memberIds:ids,authorId:myMemberId,authorUid:window.auth?.currentUser?.uid||'',author:m?.nome||me?.nome||'Carbonauta',active:true,ts:f().serverTimestamp()});$('#p46GroupOverlay').style.display='none';activeGroupId=gid;mode='groups';toast('Panelinha criada.');setTimeout(renderHeader,100)}catch(e){console.error(e);toast('Não consegui criar a panelinha.')}
}

async function loadIdentityAndSubscribe(){if(!window.auth?.currentUser||!window.db||!window.fbFns)return false;const uid=window.auth.currentUser.uid;try{const us=await f().getDoc(f().doc(window.db,'rede_users',uid));if(us.exists()){me=us.data();myMemberId=me.memberId||''}}catch(e){console.warn('P46 perfil',e)}if(!myMemberId)return false;try{if(unsubMembers)unsubMembers();unsubMembers=f().onSnapshot(f().collection(window.db,'rede_members'),snap=>{members=snap.docs.map(d=>({...d.data(),id:d.id}));renderSide();if($('#p46GroupOverlay')?.style.display==='flex')renderPeople()})}catch(e){console.warn(e)}try{if(unsubChat)unsubChat();const q=f().query(f().collection(window.db,'rede_chat'),f().orderBy('ts','asc'));unsubChat=f().onSnapshot(q,snap=>{chatDocs=snap.docs.map(d=>({...d.data(),id:d.id}));if(mode==='groups'&&!groupById(activeGroupId))activeGroupId=groupMetas()[0]?.groupId||'';renderHeader()},e=>console.warn('P46 chat',e))}catch(e){console.warn(e)}return true}

function renderPeople(){const box=$('#p46People');if(!box)return;const q=norm($('#p46PersonSearch')?.value).toLowerCase();box.innerHTML=members.filter(m=>m.status!=='inativo'&&(!q||norm(m.nome).toLowerCase().includes(q))).sort((a,b)=>norm(a.nome).localeCompare(norm(b.nome),'pt-BR')).map(m=>`<label class="p46-person"><input type="checkbox" value="${esc(m.id)}" ${m.id===myMemberId?'checked disabled':''}><span>${esc(m.nome||'Carbonauta')}</span><small>${esc(m.nivel||'')}</small></label>`).join('')}

async function createGroup(){const name=norm($('#p46GroupName')?.value);if(!name)return toast('Dê um nome para a panelinha.');const ids=Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('#p46People input:checked')).map(x=>x.value);if(!ids.includes(myMemberId))ids.push(myMemberId);if(ids.length<2)return toast('Escolha pelo menos mais uma pessoa.');const gid='g_'+Date.now().toString(36)+'_'+Math.random().toString(36).slice(2,7),m=member(myMemberId);try{await f().addDoc(f().collection(window.db,'rede_chat'),{tipo:'chat_group_meta',groupId:gid,groupName:name.slice(0,80),memberIds:ids,authorId:myMemberId,authorUid:window.auth?.currentUser?.uid||'',author:m?.nome||me?.nome||'Carbonauta',active:true,ts:f().serverTimestamp()});$('#p46GroupOverlay').style.display='none';activeGroupId=gid;mode='groups';toast('Panelinha criada.');setTimeout(renderHeader,100)}catch(e){console.error(e);toast('Não consegui criar a panelinha.')}
}

async function loadIdentityAndSubscribe(){if(!window.auth?.currentUser||!window.db||!window.fbFns)return false;const uid=window.auth.currentUser.uid;try{const us=await f().getDoc(f().doc(window.db,'rede_users',uid));if(us.exists()){me=us.data();myMemberId=me.memberId||''}}catch(e){console.warn('P46 perfil',e)}if(!myMemberId)return false;try{if(unsubMembers)unsubMembers();unsubMembers=f().onSnapshot(f().collection(window.db,'rede_members'),snap=>{members=snap.docs.map(d=>({...d.data(),id:d.id}));renderSide();if($('#p46GroupOverlay')?.style.display==='flex')renderPeople()})}catch(e){console.warn(e)}try{if(unsubChat)unsubChat();const q=f().query(f().collection(window.db,'rede_chat'),f().orderBy('ts','asc'));unsubChat=f().onSnapshot(q,snap=>{chatDocs=snap.docs.map(d=>({...d.data(),id:d.id}));if(mode==='groups'&&!groupById(activeGroupId))activeGroupId=groupMetas()[0]?.groupId||'';renderHeader()},e=>console.warn('P46 chat',e))}catch(e){console.warn(e)}return true}

function openGeneralChat(){mode='general';activeGroupId='';const view=$('#viewMensagens');if(view){view.querySelector('#p48GroupsRoot')?.remove();view.querySelectorAll('[data-p98-general-sentinel]').forEach(n=>n.remove())}ensureMount();renderHeader();document.body.classList.remove('p98-panel-active');document.body.removeAttribute('data-p48-groups')}
window.openGeneralChat=openGeneralChat;
function installOverrides(){if(typeof window.renderMensagens==='function')window.renderMensagens=()=>{renderHeader()};const oldSub=window.renderSubNav;if(typeof oldSub==='function'&&!oldSub.__p46){const wrapped=function(v){oldSub(v);setTimeout(enhanceSubNav,0)};wrapped.__p46=true;window.renderSubNav=wrapped}}

async function boot(){injectCss();if(!ensureMount())return;installOverrides();enhanceSubNav();let tries=0;while(tries++<80){if(await loadIdentityAndSubscribe())break;await new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r,250))}renderHeader();console.info('Carbonautas chat',VERSION,'carregado')}
if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',boot,{once:true});else boot();
})();

function renderPeople(){const box=$('#p46People');if(!box)return;const q=norm($('#p46PersonSearch')?.value).toLowerCase();box.innerHTML=members.filter(m=>m.status!=='inativo'&&(!q||norm(m.nome).toLowerCase().includes(q))).sort((a,b)=>norm(a.nome).localeCompare(norm(b.nome),'pt-BR')).map(m=>`<label class="p46-person"><input type="checkbox" value="${esc(m.id)}" ${m.id===myMemberId?'checked disabled':''}><span>${esc(m.nome||'Carbonauta')}</span><small>${esc(m.nivel||'')}</small></label>`).join('')}
function openGroupModal(){if(!myMemberId)return toast('Seu login precisa estar vinculado a uma pessoa.');const ov=ensureGroupModal();$('#p46GroupName').value='';$('#p46PersonSearch').value='';renderPeople();ov.style.display='flex';setTimeout(()=>$('#p46GroupName')?.focus(),40)}
async function createGroup(){const name=norm($('#p46GroupName')?.value);if(!name)return toast('Dê um nome para a panelinha.');const ids=Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('#p46People input:checked')).map(x=>x.value);if(!ids.includes(myMemberId))ids.push(myMemberId);if(ids.length<2)return toast('Escolha pelo menos mais uma pessoa.');const gid='g_'+Date.now().toString(36)+'_'+Math.random().toString(36).slice(2,7),m=member(myMemberId);try{await f().addDoc(f().collection(window.db,'rede_chat'),{tipo:'chat_group_meta',groupId:gid,groupName:name.slice(0,80),memberIds:ids,authorId:myMemberId,authorUid:window.auth?.currentUser?.uid||'',author:m?.nome||me?.nome||'Carbonauta',active:true,ts:f().serverTimestamp()});$('#p46GroupOverlay').style.display='none';activeGroupId=gid;mode='groups';toast('Panelinha criada.');setTimeout(renderHeader,100)}catch(e){console.error(e);toast('Não consegui criar a panelinha.')}
}

async function loadIdentityAndSubscribe(){if(!window.auth?.currentUser||!window.db||!window.fbFns)return false;const uid=window.auth.currentUser.uid;try{const us=await f().getDoc(f().doc(window.db,'rede_users',uid));if(us.exists()){me=us.data();myMemberId=me.memberId||''}}catch(e){console.warn('P46 perfil',e)}if(!myMemberId)return false;try{if(unsubMembers)unsubMembers();unsubMembers=f().onSnapshot(f().collection(window.db,'rede_members'),snap=>{members=snap.docs.map(d=>({...d.data(),id:d.id}));renderSide();if($('#p46GroupOverlay')?.style.display==='flex')renderPeople()})}catch(e){console.warn(e)}try{if(unsubChat)unsubChat();const q=f().query(f().collection(window.db,'rede_chat'),f().orderBy('ts','asc'));unsubChat=f().onSnapshot(q,snap=>{chatDocs=snap.docs.map(d=>({...d.data(),id:d.id}));if(mode==='groups'&&!groupById(activeGroupId))activeGroupId=groupMetas()[0]?.groupId||'';renderHeader()},e=>console.warn('P46 chat',e))}catch(e){console.warn(e)}return true}

function openGeneralChat(){mode='general';activeGroupId='';const view=$('#viewMensagens');if(view){view.querySelector('#p48GroupsRoot')?.remove();view.querySelectorAll('[data-p98-general-sentinel]').forEach(n=>n.remove())}ensureMount();renderHeader();document.body.classList.remove('p98-panel-active');document.body.removeAttribute('data-p48-groups')}
window.openGeneralChat=openGeneralChat;
function installOverrides(){if(typeof window.renderMensagens==='function')window.renderMensagens=()=>{renderHeader()};const oldSub=window.renderSubNav;if(typeof oldSub==='function'&&!oldSub.__p46){const wrapped=function(v){oldSub(v);setTimeout(enhanceSubNav,0)};wrapped.__p46=true;window.renderSubNav=wrapped}}

async function boot(){injectCss();if(!ensureMount())return;installOverrides();enhanceSubNav();let tries=0;while(tries++<80){if(await loadIdentityAndSubscribe())break;await new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r,250))}renderHeader();console.info('Carbonautas chat',VERSION,'carregado')}
if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',boot,{once:true});else boot();
})();

function clarify(){
  const head=document.querySelector('.private-head');
  if(head){
    const h=head.querySelector('h2'); if(h)h.textContent='Privadas';
    const p=head.querySelector('p'); if(p)p.textContent='Conversa direta 1:1. Assuntos de arquivos ficam na Sala do arquivo, dentro do Repositório.';
  }
  document.querySelectorAll('.repo-room-btn').forEach(b=>{
    if(!b.dataset.p47){b.dataset.p47='1';b.textContent='🗂 Conversa do arquivo';b.title='Esta conversa fica vinculada somente a este arquivo no Repositório.';}
  });
}

// Evita que um atalho antigo do Repositório leve para Privadas.
// A conversa de arquivo permanece na Sala do arquivo e só é visível ao proprietário + colaboradores.
if(typeof window.messagePublicationOwner==='function'){
  window.messagePublicationOwner=function(pubId){
    if(typeof window.openRepoFileConversationPublication==='function') return window.openRepoFileConversationPublication(pubId);
  };
}

clarify();
setInterval(clarify,1200);
console.log('Carbonautas P47 · privadas na paleta + conversa de arquivo somente no Repositório');
})();

function renderMessages(){const box=$('#p48Msgs');if(!box)return;if(!activeThreadId){box.innerHTML='<div class="p48-empty">Escolha ou crie uma panelinha.</div>';return}if(!messages.length){box.innerHTML='<div class="p48-empty">Ainda não há mensagens nesta panelinha.</div>';return}let last='';box.innerHTML=messages.map(m=>{const d=dayKey(m.createdAt),sep=d!==last?(last=d,`<div class="p48-day">${dayLabel(m.createdAt)}</div>`):'',mine=m.authorMemberId===myMemberId,a=member(m.authorMemberId),name=a?.nome||m.authorName||'Carbonauta',photo=a?.foto||'',av=photo?`<img src="${esc(photo)}">`:initials(name),quote=m.replyTo?`<div class="p48-quote"><b>${esc(m.replyTo.authorName||'')}</b> · ${esc(m.replyTo.textExcerpt||'')}</div>`:'';return `${sep}<div class="p48-row ${mine?'mine':''}" data-mid="${esc(m.id)}"><div class="p48-av">${av}</div><div class="p48-stack"><div class="p48-name">${esc(name)}</div><div class="p48-bubble">${quote}<div class="p48-text">${esc(m.text||'')}</div><div class="p48-meta"><span>${esc(m.topic||'Geral')}</span><span>${timeLabel(m.createdAt)}</span><button data-reply="${esc(m.id)}">↩ responder</button></div></div></div></div>`}).join('');box.querySelectorAll('[data-reply]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>setReply(b.dataset.reply));box.scrollTop=box.scrollHeight}

async function createGroup(){
  const name=norm($('#p48Name')?.value);
  if(!name)return toast('Dê um nome para a panelinha.');
  const ids=Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('#p48People input:checked')).map(x=>x.value);
  if(!ids.includes(myMemberId))ids.push(myMemberId);
  const uniq=[...new Set(ids)];
  if(uniq.length<2)return toast('Escolha pelo menos mais uma pessoa.');
  if(uniq.length>60)return toast('Uma panelinha pode ter até 60 participantes.');
  const meM=member(myMemberId),groupName=name.slice(0,80),createdByName=meM?.nome||me?.nome||'Carbonauta',btn=$('#p48Create');
  if(btn){btn.disabled=true;btn.textContent='Criando…'}
  try{
    const ref=await f().addDoc(f().collection(window.db,'rede_group_threads'),{groupName,memberIds:uniq,createdByUid:window.auth.currentUser.uid,createdByMemberId:myMemberId,createdByName,active:true,createdAt:f().serverTimestamp(),updatedAt:f().serverTimestamp()});
    const localThread={id:ref.id,groupName,memberIds:uniq,createdByUid:window.auth.currentUser.uid,createdByMemberId:myMemberId,createdByName,active:true,lastMessage:'',createdAt:{toMillis:()=>Date.now()},updatedAt:{toMillis:()=>Date.now()}};
    threads=[localThread,...threads.filter(x=>x.id!==ref.id)];
    $('#p48GroupOverlay').style.display='none';
    activeThreadId=ref.id;
    mount();renderList();renderHead();renderMessages();subscribeMessages(ref.id);
    $('#p48Shell')?.classList.add('chat-open');
    toast('Panelinha criada.');
    setTimeout(()=>{subscribe().catch(e=>console.warn('P205 resubscribe',e))},300);
  }catch(e){
    console.error('P48 create group',e);showRulesWarning();
    toast(e?.code==='permission-denied'?'O Firestore recusou a criação. Falta publicar as regras atuais.':'Não consegui criar a panelinha: '+(e?.message||'erro desconhecido'));
  }finally{
    if(btn){btn.disabled=false;btn.textContent='Criar panelinha'}
  }
}
function showRulesWarning(){$('#p48RulesWarn')?.classList.add('show')}

async function createGroup(){
  const name=norm($('#p48Name')?.value);
  if(!name)return toast('Dê um nome para a panelinha.');
  const ids=Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('#p48People input:checked')).map(x=>x.value);
  if(!ids.includes(myMemberId))ids.push(myMemberId);
  const uniq=[...new Set(ids)];
  if(uniq.length<2)return toast('Escolha pelo menos mais uma pessoa.');
  if(uniq.length>60)return toast('Uma panelinha pode ter até 60 participantes.');
  const meM=member(myMemberId),groupName=name.slice(0,80),createdByName=meM?.nome||me?.nome||'Carbonauta',btn=$('#p48Create');
  if(btn){btn.disabled=true;btn.textContent='Criando…'}
  try{
    const ref=await f().addDoc(f().collection(window.db,'rede_group_threads'),{groupName,memberIds:uniq,createdByUid:window.auth.currentUser.uid,createdByMemberId:myMemberId,createdByName,active:true,createdAt:f().serverTimestamp(),updatedAt:f().serverTimestamp()});
    const localThread={id:ref.id,groupName,memberIds:uniq,createdByUid:window.auth.currentUser.uid,createdByMemberId:myMemberId,createdByName,active:true,lastMessage:'',createdAt:{toMillis:()=>Date.now()},updatedAt:{toMillis:()=>Date.now()}};
    threads=[localThread,...threads.filter(x=>x.id!==ref.id)];
    $('#p48GroupOverlay').style.display='none';
    activeThreadId=ref.id;
    mount();renderList();renderHead();renderMessages();subscribeMessages(ref.id);
    $('#p48Shell')?.classList.add('chat-open');
    toast('Panelinha criada.');
    setTimeout(()=>{subscribe().catch(e=>console.warn('P205 resubscribe',e))},300);
  }catch(e){
    console.error('P48 create group',e);showRulesWarning();
    toast(e?.code==='permission-denied'?'O Firestore recusou a criação. Falta publicar as regras atuais.':'Não consegui criar a panelinha: '+(e?.message||'erro desconhecido'));
  }finally{
    if(btn){btn.disabled=false;btn.textContent='Criar panelinha'}
  }
}

async function deletePrivateMessage(id){const msg=privateMessagesState.find(x=>x.id===id);if(!msg||msg.senderUid!==privateUid())return;if(!confirm('Excluir esta mensagem da conversa?'))return;const mark='[[CARBONAUTAS_IMAGE_V1]]',raw=String(msg.text||''),rest=raw.startsWith(mark)?raw.slice(mark.length):'',nl=rest.indexOf('\n'),imageUrl=rest?(nl>=0?rest.slice(0,nl):rest):'';try{await FB().deleteDoc(FB().doc(window.db,'rede_private_threads',privateActiveThreadId,'messages',id));const isLast=privateMessagesState[privateMessagesState.length-1]?.id===id;if(isLast){const prev=privateMessagesState[privateMessagesState.length-2];await FB().updateDoc(FB().doc(window.db,'rede_private_threads',privateActiveThreadId),{lastMessage:prev?.text?.slice(0,160)||'Mensagem excluída'})}const failed=imageUrl?await deleteStorageArtifacts({url:imageUrl}):[];toast(failed.length?'Mensagem excluída, mas a imagem não pôde ser removida do Storage.':'Mensagem excluída.')}catch(e){console.error(e);toast('Não foi possível excluir a mensagem.')}}

const VIEW_GROUPS={
  painel:{views:['painel']},
  pubs:{views:['pubs']},
  pessoas:{views:['rede','track'],labels:{rede:'Rede',track:'Acompanhamento'}},
  agenda:{views:['crono']},
  conversas:{views:['mensagens','conversas'],labels:{mensagens:'Fofoca geral',conversas:'Privadas'},badges:{mensagens:'msgBadge',conversas:'privateBadge'}},
  mural:{views:['mural','fotos','destaques'],labels:{mural:'Mural',fotos:'Fotos',destaques:'Destaques'},badges:{mural:'muralBadge'}},
  lab:{views:['lab']},
  medalhas:{views:['medalhas']},
  jogo:{views:['jogo']}
};
function groupOfView(v){return Object.keys(VIEW_GROUPS).find(g=>VIEW_GROUPS[g].views.includes(v))||v}
function renderSubNav(v){
  const nav=$('#subNav');if(!nav)return;
  const g=VIEW_GROUPS[groupOfView(v)];
  if(!g||g.views.length<2){nav.style.display='none';nav.innerHTML='';return}
  nav.style.display='flex';
  nav.innerHTML=g.views.map(x=>{const b=g.badges?.[x],bd=b?$('#'+b):null;const n=bd&&bd.style.display!=='none'?`<span class="badge">${esc(bd.textContent)}</span>`:'';return `<button type="button" class="${x===v?'on':''}" data-sub-view="${x}">${esc(g.labels[x])}${n}</button>`}).join('');
  /* P203H1: Panelinhas é uma aba real da navegação. Antes ela dependia de um
     patch posterior recriar o botão; como este script é type=module, aquela
     ponte podia desaparecer toda vez que renderSubNav reconstruía o HTML. */
  if(groupOfView(v)==='conversas'){
    const priv=nav.querySelector('[data-sub-view="conversas"]');
    if(priv&&!nav.querySelector('.p46-panel-btn')){
      const pan=document.createElement('button');
      pan.type='button';
      pan.className='p46-panel-btn';
      pan.textContent='Conversas em panelinha';
      pan.setAttribute('aria-label','Conversas em panelinha');
      pan.onclick=()=>{ if(typeof window.openPanelinhas==='function') window.openPanelinhas(); };
      nav.insertBefore(pan,priv);
    }
  }
  nav.querySelectorAll('[data-sub-view]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>switchView(b.dataset.subView));
}
function switchView(v){
  currentView=v;
  document.body.dataset.view=v;
  const grp=groupOfView(v);
  $$('.view').forEach(el=>el.classList.remove('on'));
  $$('.tab').forEach(el=>el.classList.toggle('on',(el.dataset.group||el.dataset.view)===grp));
  renderSubNav(v);
  const map={painel:'viewPainel',rede:'viewRede',crono:'viewCrono',track:'viewTrack',lab:'viewLab',feed:'viewFeed',mensagens:'viewMensagens',conversas:'viewConversas',mural:'viewMural',fotos:'viewFotos',destaques:'viewDestaques',pubs:'viewPubs',medalhas:'viewMedalhas',jogo:'viewJogo'};
  map[v] && $('#'+map[v]).classList.add('on');
  if(v==='painel') renderPainel();
  if(v==='rede') renderAll();
  if(v==='crono') renderCrono();
  if(v==='track') renderTrack();
  if(v==='lab'){ renderLab(); checkScienceEngine(); }
  if(v==='feed') renderFeed();
  if(v==='mensagens'){renderMensagens();markQuickSeen();}
  if(v==='conversas'){renderPrivateThreads();updatePrivateBadge();const t=state.privateThreads.find(x=>x.id===privateActiveThreadId);if(t)markPrivateThreadRead(t).catch(()=>{});}
  if(v==='mural'){ renderMural(); markChatSeen(); }
  if(v==='fotos') renderFotos();
  if(v==='pubs'){ renderPubs(); checkScienceEngine(); }
  if(v==='medalhas') renderMedals();
  $('#side').classList.remove('open');
  if($('#appMenuBackdrop')){ $('#appMenuBackdrop').classList.remove('open'); $('#appMenuBackdrop').setAttribute('aria-hidden','true'); }
}

  renderStats(); renderLegend(); renderLinhaFilters(); renderProgramaFilter(); renderGraph();
}
function clearFilters(){
  hiddenNiveis.clear(); hiddenLinhas.clear();
  $('#search').value=''; $('#fPrograma').value=''; $('#fStatus').value='';
  renderAll();
}

  painel:{views:['painel']},
  pubs:{views:['pubs']},
  pessoas:{views:['rede','track'],labels:{rede:'Rede',track:'Acompanhamento'}},
  agenda:{views:['crono']},
  conversas:{views:['mensagens','conversas'],labels:{mensagens:'Fofoca geral',conversas:'Privadas'},badges:{mensagens:'msgBadge',conversas:'privateBadge'}},
  mural:{views:['mural','fotos','destaques'],labels:{mural:'Mural',fotos:'Fotos',destaques:'Destaques'},badges:{mural:'muralBadge'}},
  lab:{views:['lab']},
  medalhas:{views:['medalhas']},
  jogo:{views:['jogo']}
};
function groupOfView(v){return Object.keys(VIEW_GROUPS).find(g=>VIEW_GROUPS[g].views.includes(v))||v}
function renderSubNav(v){
  const nav=$('#subNav');if(!nav)return;
  const g=VIEW_GROUPS[groupOfView(v)];
  if(!g||g.views.length<2){nav.style.display='none';nav.innerHTML='';return}
  nav.style.display='flex';
  nav.innerHTML=g.views.map(x=>{const b=g.badges?.[x],bd=b?$('#'+b):null;const n=bd&&bd.style.display!=='none'?`<span class="badge">${esc(bd.textContent)}</span>`:'';return `<button type="button" class="${x===v?'on':''}" data-sub-view="${x}">${esc(g.labels[x])}${n}</button>`}).join('');
  /* P203H1: Panelinhas é uma aba real da navegação. Antes ela dependia de um
     patch posterior recriar o botão; como este script é type=module, aquela
     ponte podia desaparecer toda vez que renderSubNav reconstruía o HTML. */
  if(groupOfView(v)==='conversas'){
    const priv=nav.querySelector('[data-sub-view="conversas"]');
    if(priv&&!nav.querySelector('.p46-panel-btn')){
      const pan=document.createElement('button');
      pan.type='button';
      pan.className='p46-panel-btn';
      pan.textContent='Conversas em panelinha';
      pan.setAttribute('aria-label','Conversas em panelinha');
      pan.onclick=()=>{ if(typeof window.openPanelinhas==='function') window.openPanelinhas(); };
      nav.insertBefore(pan,priv);
    }
  }
  nav.querySelectorAll('[data-sub-view]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>switchView(b.dataset.subView));
}
function switchView(v){
  currentView=v;
  document.body.dataset.view=v;
  const grp=groupOfView(v);
  $$('.view').forEach(el=>el.classList.remove('on'));
  $$('.tab').forEach(el=>el.classList.toggle('on',(el.dataset.group||el.dataset.view)===grp));
  renderSubNav(v);
  const map={painel:'viewPainel',rede:'viewRede',crono:'viewCrono',track:'viewTrack',lab:'viewLab',feed:'viewFeed',mensagens:'viewMensagens',conversas:'viewConversas',mural:'viewMural',fotos:'viewFotos',destaques:'viewDestaques',pubs:'viewPubs',medalhas:'viewMedalhas',jogo:'viewJogo'};
  map[v] && $('#'+map[v]).classList.add('on');
  if(v==='painel') renderPainel();
  if(v==='rede') renderAll();
  if(v==='crono') renderCrono();
  if(v==='track') renderTrack();
  if(v==='lab'){ renderLab(); checkScienceEngine(); }
  if(v==='feed') renderFeed();
  if(v==='mensagens'){renderMensagens();markQuickSeen();}
  if(v==='conversas'){renderPrivateThreads();updatePrivateBadge();const t=state.privateThreads.find(x=>x.id===privateActiveThreadId);if(t)markPrivateThreadRead(t).catch(()=>{});}
  if(v==='mural'){ renderMural(); markChatSeen(); }
  if(v==='fotos') renderFotos();
  if(v==='pubs'){ renderPubs(); checkScienceEngine(); }
  if(v==='medalhas') renderMedals();
  $('#side').classList.remove('open');
  if($('#appMenuBackdrop')){ $('#appMenuBackdrop').classList.remove('open'); $('#appMenuBackdrop').setAttribute('aria-hidden','true'); }
}

function openMobileMuralSheet(){

--- occurrence 4 at char 814840 ---
  let m=s.match(/openActivity\(null\s*,\s*['\"]([^'\"]+)['\"]\)/);if(m)return m[1];
    m=s.match(/openDossier\(['\"]([^'\"]+)['\"]\)/);if(m)return m[1];
    m=s.match(/openMemberRepository\(['\"]([^'\"]+)['\"]\)/);if(m)return m[1];
  }
  return'';
}

function openCustomPeriod(memberId,type){
  const section=[...document.querySelectorAll('#trackGrid .track-person')].find(x=>memberIdFromSection(x)===memberId);
  const reg=[...(section?.querySelectorAll('button[onclick]')||[])].find(x=>(x.getAttribute('onclick')||'').includes('openActivity(null'));
  if(!reg)return;
  reg.click();
  setTimeout(()=>{
    addSelectOptions();
    const sel=$('#activityType');if(sel){sel.value=type;sel.dispatchEvent(new Event('change',{bubbles:true}))}
    const name=$('#activityName');if(name&&!name.value)name.value=TYPES[type]?.title||'';
    const status=$('#activityStatus');if(status&&!status.value)status.value='andamento';
    $('#activityStart')?.focus();
  },80);
}

function enhancePicker(){
  const p=$('#p44PeriodPicker'),box=p?.querySelector('.p44-options');if(!p||!box)return;
  const defs=[
    ['ic','🔬 IC','Iniciação Científica · início e fim'],
    ['ie','🤝 IE','Iniciação à Extensão · início e fim'],
    ['it','💡 IT','Iniciação Tecnológica · início e fim'],
    ['projeto_periodo','📁 Projeto','Datas do projeto de pesquisa']
  ];
  defs.forEach(([type,label,help])=>{
    if(box.querySelector(`[data-p45type="${type}"]`))return;
    const b=document.createElement('button');b.type='button';b.className='p44-option';b.dataset.p45type=type;b.innerHTML=`${label}<small>${help}</small>`;
    b.addEventListener('click',()=>{
      const id=p.dataset.memberId||'';p.style.display='none';p.dataset.memberId='';if(id)openCustomPeriod(id,type);
    });
    box.appendChild(b);
  });
  const help=p.querySelector('.p44-help');if(help)help.textContent='Adicione períodos acadêmicos e as datas dos projetos. O próprio aluno pode registrar os períodos dele; a coordenação pode registrar para qualquer pessoa.';
}

function boot(){
  registerTypes();addSelectOptions();enhancePicker();
  setTimeout(()=>{registerTypes();addSelectOptions();enhancePicker();try{if(typeof renderTrack==='function')renderTrack()}catch(e){}},400);
  setInterval(()=>{registerTypes();addSelectOptions();enhancePicker()},1800);
  console.info('Carbonautas períodos acadêmicos',VERSION,'carregado');
}
if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',boot,{once:true});else boot();
})();

<script data-src="chat-whatsapp-p46.js">
/* Carbonautas P46 · chat estilo WhatsApp: Fofoca geral + Panelinhas + respostas */
(function(){
'use strict';
const VERSION='P46';
const $=s=>document.querySelector(s);
const esc=s=>String(s??'').replace(/[&<>"']/g,c=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[c]));
const norm=s=>String(s||'').trim();
const PRESET_TOPICS=['Geral','Campo','Laboratório','Dados','Artigos','Eventos','Prazos'];
let mode='general';
let activeTopic='Todos';
let composerTopic='Geral';
let activeGroupId='';
let replyTarget=null;
let chatDocs=[];
let members=[];
let me=null;
let myMemberId='';
let unsubChat=null,unsubMembers=null;
let mounted=false;

function f(){return window.fbFns}
function toast(msg){if(typeof window.toast==='function')window.toast(msg);else console.log('[Carbonautas]',msg)}
function millis(ts){return ts?.toMillis?ts.toMillis():(ts?.seconds?ts.seconds*1000:+new Date(ts||0))}
function timeLabel(ts){const d=new Date(millis(ts)||Date.now());return d.toLocaleTimeString('pt-BR',{hour:'2-digit',minute:'2-digit'})}
function dayKey(ts){const d=new Date(millis(ts)||Date.now());return d.toLocaleDateString('pt-BR',{year:'numeric',month:'2-digit',day:'2-digit'})}
function dayLabel(ts){const d=new Date(millis(ts)||Date.now()),today=new Date(),y=new Date();y.setDate(today.getDate()-1);const same=(a,b)=>a.toDateString()===b.toDateString();return same(d,today)?'Hoje':same(d,y)?'Ontem':d.toLocaleDateString('pt-BR',{day:'2-digit',month:'short',year:d.getFullYear()===today.getFullYear()?undefined:'numeric'})}
function initials(name){return norm(name).split(/\s+/).slice(0,2).map(x=>x[0]?.toUpperCase()||'').join('')||'?'}
function member(id){return members.find(x=>x.id===id)||null}
function groupMetas(){return chatDocs.filter(x=>x.tipo==='chat_group_meta'&&x.active!==false&&Array.isArray(x.memberIds)&&x.memberIds.includes(myMemberId))}
function groupById(id){return groupMetas().find(x=>x.groupId===id)||null}
function generalMessages(){return chatDocs.filter(x=>x.tipo!=='recado'&&x.tipo!=='chat_group_meta'&&x.tipo!=='chat_group_message'&&x.tipo!=='mural')}
function groupMessages(id){return chatDocs.filter(x=>x.tipo==='chat_group_message'&&x.groupId===id)}

function bindUI(){$('#p48New').onclick=openModal;$('#p48Back').onclick=()=>$('#p48Shell').classList.remove('chat-open');$('#p48Send').onclick=sendMessage;$('#p48CancelReply').onclick=()=>{replyTarget=null;renderReply()};const inp=$('#p48Input');inp.oninput=()=>{inp.style.height='auto';inp.style.height=Math.min(120,inp.scrollHeight)+'px'};inp.onkeydown=e=>{if(e.key==='Enter'&&!e.shiftKey){e.preventDefault();sendMessage()}}}
function renderList(){const box=$('#p48List');if(!box)return;if(!threads.length){box.innerHTML='<div class="p48-empty">Nenhuma panelinha ainda.<br>Crie uma para começar.</div>';return}box.innerHTML=threads.map(t=>{const n=(t.memberIds||[]).length,last=t.lastMessage||'';return `<button class="p48-thread ${t.id===activeThreadId?'on':''}" data-g="${esc(t.id)}"><span class="p48-ico">👥</span><span class="p48-txt"><b>${esc(t.groupName||'Panelinha')}</b><small>${n} participantes${last?' · '+esc(last):''}</small></span></button>`}).join('');box.querySelectorAll('[data-g]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>selectThread(b.dataset.g))}
function selectThread(id){activeThreadId=id;renderList();renderHead();subscribeMessages(id);$('#p48Shell')?.classList.add('chat-open')}
function renderHead(){const t=thread(),title=$('#p48Title'),sub=$('#p48Sub'),comp=$('#p48Compose');if(!title)return;if(!t){title.textContent='Panelinhas';sub.textContent='Escolha um grupo';comp.style.display='none';return}title.textContent=t.groupName||'Panelinha';sub.textContent=`${(t.memberIds||[]).length} participantes · conversa privada do grupo`;comp.style.display='block'}
function renderMessages(){const box=$('#p48Msgs');if(!box)return;if(!activeThreadId){box.innerHTML='<div class="p48-empty">Escolha ou crie uma panelinha.</div>';return}if(!messages.length){box.innerHTML='<div class="p48-empty">Ainda não há mensagens nesta panelinha.</div>';return}let last='';box.innerHTML=messages.map(m=>{const d=dayKey(m.createdAt),sep=d!==last?(last=d,`<div class="p48-day">${dayLabel(m.createdAt)}</div>`):'',mine=m.authorMemberId===myMemberId,a=member(m.authorMemberId),name=a?.nome||m.authorName||'Carbonauta',photo=a?.foto||'',av=photo?`<img src="${esc(photo)}">`:initials(name),quote=m.replyTo?`<div class="p48-quote"><b>${esc(m.replyTo.authorName||'')}</b> · ${esc(m.replyTo.textExcerpt||'')}</div>`:'';return `${sep}<div class="p48-row ${mine?'mine':''}" data-mid="${esc(m.id)}"><div class="p48-av">${av}</div><div class="p48-stack"><div class="p48-name">${esc(name)}</div><div class="p48-bubble">${quote}<div class="p48-text">${esc(m.text||'')}</div><div class="p48-meta"><span>${esc(m.topic||'Geral')}</span><span>${timeLabel(m.createdAt)}</span><button data-reply="${esc(m.id)}">↩ responder</button></div></div></div></div>`}).join('');box.querySelectorAll('[data-reply]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>setReply(b.dataset.reply));box.scrollTop=box.scrollHeight}
function setReply(id){const m=messages.find(x=>x.id===id);if(!m)return;replyTarget={messageId:id,authorName:member(m.authorMemberId)?.nome||m.authorName||'Carbonauta',textExcerpt:(m.text||'').slice(0,130)};renderReply();$('#p48Input')?.focus()}
function renderReply(){const r=$('#p48Replying'),t=$('#p48ReplyText');if(!r||!t)return;if(replyTarget){t.innerHTML=`<b>Respondendo a ${esc(replyTarget.authorName)}</b> · ${esc(replyTarget.textExcerpt)}`;r.classList.add('show')}else r.classList.remove('show')}
async function sendMessage(){const t=thread(),inp=$('#p48Input'),text=norm(inp?.value);if(!t||!text)return;if(text.length>5000)return toast('A mensagem pode ter no máximo 5.000 caracteres.');const meM=member(myMemberId);try{const payload={authorUid:window.auth.currentUser.uid,authorMemberId:myMemberId,authorName:meM?.nome||me?.nome||'Carbonauta',text,topic:$('#p48Topic')?.value||'Geral',replyTo:replyTarget?{...replyTarget}:null,createdAt:f().serverTimestamp()};await f().addDoc(f().collection(window.db,'rede_group_threads',t.id,'messages'),payload);await f().updateDoc(f().doc(window.db,'rede_group_threads',t.id),{updatedAt:f().serverTimestamp(),lastMessage:text.slice(0,160),lastSenderId:myMemberId});inp.value='';inp.style.height='auto';replyTarget=null;renderReply()}catch(e){console.error('P48 send',e);showRulesWarning();toast(e?.code==='permission-denied'?'Publique as regras P48 do Firestore para usar as panelinhas.':'Não consegui enviar a mensagem.') }}
function ensureModal(){let o=$('#p48GroupOverlay');if(o)return o;o=document.createElement('div');o.id='p48GroupOverlay';o.innerHTML=`<div class="p48-modal"><div class="p48-mh"><b>Nova conversa em panelinha</b><button id="p48Close">×</button></div><div class="p48-mb"><input id="p48Name" type="text" placeholder="Nome da panelinha — ex.: Artigo do Pantanal"><input id="p48Search" type="text" placeholder="Buscar participante…"><div class="p48-people" id="p48People"></div><p style="font-size:11px;color:#70838a">🔐 Apenas as pessoas marcadas poderão carregar as mensagens desta panelinha.</p></div><div class="p48-mf"><button id="p48Cancel">Cancelar</button><button class="primary" id="p48Create">Criar panelinha</button></div></div>`;document.body.appendChild(o);$('#p48Close').onclick=$('#p48Cancel').onclick=()=>o.style.display='none';$('#p48Search').oninput=renderPeople;$('#p48Create').onclick=createGroup;o.onclick=e=>{if(e.target===o)o.style.display='none'};return o}
function renderPeople(){const box=$('#p48People');if(!box)return;const q=norm($('#p48Search')?.value).toLowerCase();box.innerHTML=members.filter(m=>m.status!=='inativo'&&(!q||norm(m.nome).toLowerCase().includes(q))).sort((a,b)=>norm(a.nome).localeCompare(norm(b.nome),'pt-BR')).map(m=>`<label class="p48-person"><input type="checkbox" value="${esc(m.id)}" ${m.id===myMemberId?'checked disabled':''}><span>${esc(m.nome||'Carbonauta')}</span><small>${esc(m.nivel||'')}</small></label>`).join('')}

async function createGroup(){
  const name=norm($('#p48Name')?.value);
  if(!name)return toast('Dê um nome para a panelinha.');
  const ids=Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('#p48People input:checked')).map(x=>x.value);
  if(!ids.includes(myMemberId))ids.push(myMemberId);
  const uniq=[...new Set(ids)];
  if(uniq.length<2)return toast('Escolha pelo menos mais uma pessoa.');
  if(uniq.length>60)return toast('Uma panelinha pode ter até 60 participantes.');
  const meM=member(myMemberId),groupName=name.slice(0,80),createdByName=meM?.nome||me?.nome||'Carbonauta',btn=$('#p48Create');
  if(btn){btn.disabled=true;btn.textContent='Criando…'}
  try{
    const ref=await f().addDoc(f().collection(window.db,'rede_group_threads'),{groupName,memberIds:uniq,createdByUid:window.auth.currentUser.uid,createdByMemberId:myMemberId,createdByName,active:true,createdAt:f().serverTimestamp(),updatedAt:f().serverTimestamp()});
    const localThread={id:ref.id,groupName,memberIds:uniq,createdByUid:window.auth.currentUser.uid,createdByMemberId:myMemberId,createdByName,active:true,lastMessage:'',createdAt:{toMillis:()=>Date.now()},updatedAt:{toMillis:()=>Date.now()}};
    threads=[localThread,...threads.filter(x=>x.id!==ref.id)];
    $('#p48GroupOverlay').style.display='none';
    activeThreadId=ref.id;
    mount();renderList();renderHead();renderMessages();subscribeMessages(ref.id);
    $('#p48Shell')?.classList.add('chat-open');
    toast('Panelinha criada.');
    setTimeout(()=>{subscribe().catch(e=>console.warn('P205 resubscribe',e))},300);
  }catch(e){
    console.error('P48 create group',e);showRulesWarning();
    toast(e?.code==='permission-denied'?'O Firestore recusou a criação. Falta publicar as regras atuais.':'Não consegui criar a panelinha: '+(e?.message||'erro desconhecido'));
  }finally{
    if(btn){btn.disabled=false;btn.textContent='Criar panelinha'}
  }
}
function showRulesWarning(){$('#p48RulesWarn')?.classList.add('show')}
function subscribeMessages(id){if(unsubMessages){unsubMessages();unsubMessages=null}messages=[];renderMessages();if(!id)return;try{const q=f().query(f().collection(window.db,'rede_group_threads',id,'messages'),f().orderBy('createdAt','asc'));unsubMessages=f().onSnapshot(q,s=>{messages=s.docs.map(d=>({...d.data(),id:d.id}));renderMessages()},e=>{console.error('P48 messages',e);if(e?.code==='permission-denied')showRulesWarning()})}catch(e){console.error(e)}}
async function identity(){if(!window.auth?.currentUser||!window.db||!window.fbFns)return false;try{const s=await f().getDoc(f().doc(window.db,'rede_users',window.auth.currentUser.uid));if(s.exists()){me=s.data();myMemberId=me.memberId||''}}catch(e){console.warn('P48 profile',e)}return !!myMemberId}
async function subscribe(){if(!await identity())return false;try{if(unsubMembers)unsubMembers();unsubMembers=f().onSnapshot(f().collection(window.db,'rede_members'),s=>{members=s.docs.map(d=>({...d.data(),id:d.id}));if($('#p48GroupOverlay')?.style.display==='flex')renderPeople()})}catch(e){console.warn(e)}try{if(unsubThreads)unsubThreads();const q=f().query(f().collection(window.db,'rede_group_threads'),f().where('memberIds','array-contains',myMemberId));unsubThreads=f().onSnapshot(q,s=>{threads=s.docs.map(d=>({...d.data(),id:d.id})).filter(x=>x.active!==false).sort((a,b)=>millis(b.updatedAt||b.createdAt)-millis(a.updatedAt||a.createdAt));if(activeThreadId&&!threads.some(x=>x.id===activeThreadId)){activeThreadId='';subscribeMessages('')}if(active){mount();renderList();renderHead();renderMessages()}},e=>{console.error('P48 threads',e);if(e?.code==='permission-denied')showRulesWarning()})}catch(e){console.warn(e)}return true}
function openPanelinhas(){active=true;if(typeof window.switchView==='function'&&document.body.dataset.view!=='mensagens')window.switchView('mensagens');setTimeout(()=>{mount();renderList();if(!activeThreadId&&threads.length)activeThreadId=threads[0].id;renderHead();renderList();if(activeThreadId)subscribeMessages(activeThreadId)},40)}
function leave(){active=false;if(unsubMessages){unsubMessages();unsubMessages=null}document.body.removeAttribute('data-p48-groups');document.body.classList.remove('p98-panel-active');document.querySelectorAll('[data-p98-general-sentinel]').forEach(n=>n.remove());$('#p48GroupsRoot')?.remove()}
document.addEventListener('click',e=>{const pan=e.target.closest?.('.p46-panel-btn');if(pan){e.preventDefault();e.stopImmediatePropagation();openPanelinhas();return}const oldNew=e.target.closest?.('#p46NewGroup');if(oldNew){e.preventDefault();e.stopImmediatePropagation();openPanelinhas();setTimeout(openModal,50);return}const general=e.target.closest?.('#subNav [data-sub-view="mensagens"],#subNav [data-sub-view="conversas"]');if(general&&!general.classList.contains('p46-panel-btn'))leave()},true);
window.openPanelinhas=openPanelinhas; window.closePanelinhas=leave; window.openNewPanelinha=()=>{openPanelinhas();setTimeout(openModal,80)};
async function boot(){injectCss();let tries=0;while(tries++<80){if(await subscribe())break;await new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r,250))}setInterval(()=>{if(active){if(!$('#p48GroupsRoot'))mount();markTabs()}},700);console.info('Carbonautas panelinhas',VERSION,'carregado')}
if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',boot,{once:true});else boot();
})();

    body[data-view="conversas"] #subNav .p46-panel-btn{
      background:#fff!important;color:#17313d!important;border-color:#cfdde1!important;
    }
    body[data-view="conversas"] #subNav [data-sub-view="conversas"]{
      background:#0d8192!important;color:#fff!important;border-color:#0d8192!important;
    }
  `;
}

function isPanelinhaMounted(){
  const root=document.querySelector('#viewMensagens #p48GroupsRoot');
  if(!root)return false;
  /* Só vale como panelinha ativa quando a própria view de mensagens está ativa. */
  return document.body.dataset.view==='mensagens';
}

function syncTabs(){
  const nav=document.getElementById('subNav');
  if(!nav)return;
  const gen=nav.querySelector('[data-sub-view="mensagens"]');
  const pan=nav.querySelector('.p46-panel-btn');
  const priv=nav.querySelector('[data-sub-view="conversas"]');
  if(!gen&&!pan&&!priv)return;

  const view=document.body.dataset.view||'';
  if(view==='conversas'){
    gen?.classList.remove('on');
    pan?.classList.remove('on');
    priv?.classList.add('on');
    return;
  }
  if(view==='mensagens'){
    priv?.classList.remove('on');
    if(isPanelinhaMounted()){
      gen?.classList.remove('on');
      pan?.classList.add('on');
    }else{
      pan?.classList.remove('on');
      gen?.classList.add('on');
    }
  }
}

function scheduleSync(){
  if(queued)return;queued=true;
  requestAnimationFrame(()=>{queued=false;syncTabs()});
}

function bindClicks(){
  if(document.documentElement.dataset.p127TabsBound==='1')return;
  document.documentElement.dataset.p127TabsBound='1';
  document.addEventListener('click',e=>{
    if(!e.target.closest?.('#subNav button'))return;
    setTimeout(syncTabs,0);
    setTimeout(syncTabs,35);
  },true);
}

function boot(){
  installStyle();bindClicks();syncTabs();
  const nav=document.getElementById('subNav');
  if(nav&&!nav.dataset.p127Observed){
    nav.dataset.p127Observed='1';
    new MutationObserver(scheduleSync).observe(nav,{childList:true,subtree:true,attributes:true,attributeFilter:['cla

sabled=false;btn.textContent='Criar panelinha'}
  }
}
function showRulesWarning(){$('#p48RulesWarn')?.classList.add('show')}
function subscribeMessages(id){if(unsubMessages){unsubMessages();unsubMessages=null}messages=[];renderMessages();if(!id)return;try{const q=f().query(f().collection(window.db,'rede_group_threads',id,'messages'),f().orderBy('createdAt','asc'));unsubMessages=f().onSnapshot(q,s=>{messages=s.docs.map(d=>({...d.data(),id:d.id}));renderMessages()},e=>{console.error('P48 messages',e);if(e?.code==='permission-denied')showRulesWarning()})}catch(e){console.error(e)}}

<script data-src="tabs-stability-p50.js">
/* Carbonautas P127 · estado correto das abas de conversa.
   Corrige o bug em que "Conversas em panelinha" permanecia verde ao abrir Privadas.
   A aba ativa passa a seguir a tela REAL aberta, e não apenas a existência de DOM escondido. */
(function(){
'use strict';
if(window.__CARBONAUTAS_TABS_P127)return;
window.__CARBONAUTAS_TABS_P127=true;
const BUILD='P127';
let queued=false;

function installStyle(){
  let st=document.getElementById('p50TabsStyle');
  if(!st){st=document.createElement('style');st.id='p50TabsStyle';document.head.appendChild(st)}
  st.textContent=`
    #subNav button{transition:none!important;animation:none!important;}

    /* padrão: qualquer botão não ativo fica branco */
    #subNav [data-sub-view="mensagens"],
    #subNav .p46-panel-btn,
    #subNav [data-sub-view="conversas"]{
      background:#fff!important;
      color:#17313d!important;
      border-color:#cfdde1!important;
      box-shadow:none!important;
      transform:none!important;
    }

    /* somente a classe ON recebe o verde */
    #subNav [data-sub-view="mensagens"].on,
    #subNav .p46-panel-btn.on,
    #subNav [data-sub-view="conversas"].on{
      background:#0d8192!important;
      color:#fff!important;
      border-color:#0d8192!important;
      box-shadow:none!important;
      transform:none!important;
    }

    /* trava de segurança: em Privadas, SOMENTE Privadas pode ficar verde */
    body[data-view="conversas"] #subNav [data-sub-view="mensagens"],
    body[data-view="conversas"] #subNav .p46-panel-btn{
      background:#fff!important;color:#17313d!important;border-color:#cfdde1!important;
    }
    body[data-view="conversas"] #subNav [data-sub-view="conversas"]{
      background:#0d8192!important;color:#fff!important;border-color:#0d8192!important;
    }
  `;
}

function isPanelinhaMounted(){
  const root=document.querySelector('#viewMensagens #p48GroupsRoot');
  if(!root)return false;
  /* Só vale como panelinha ativa quando a própria view de mensagens está ativa. */
  return document.body.dataset.view==='mensagens';
}

function syncTabs(){
  const nav=document.getElementById('subNav');
  if(!nav)return;
  const gen=nav.querySelector('[data-sub-view="mensagens"]');
  const pan=nav.querySelector('.p46-panel-btn');
  const priv=nav.querySelector('[data-sub-view="conversas"]');
  if(!gen&&!pan&&!priv)return;

  const view=document.body.dataset.view||'';
  if(view==='conversas'){
    gen?.classList.remove('on');
    pan?.classList.remove('on');
    priv?.classList.add('on');
    return;
  }
  if(view==='mensagens'){
    priv?.classList.remove('on');
    if(isPanelinhaMounted()){
      gen?.classList.remove('on');
      pan?.classList.add('on');
    }else{
      pan?.classList.remove('on');
      gen?.classList.add('on');
    }
  }
}

function scheduleSync(){
  if(queued)return;queued=true;
  requestAnimationFrame(()=>{queued=false;syncTabs()});
}

function bindClicks(){
  if(document.documentElement.dataset.p127TabsBound==='1')return;
  document.documentElement.dataset.p127TabsBound='1';
  document.addEventListener('click',e=>{
    if(!e.target.closest?.('#subNav button'))return;
    setTimeout(syncTabs,0);
    setTimeout(syncTabs,35);
  },true);
}

function boot(){
  installStyle();bindClicks();syncTabs();
  const nav=document.getElementById('subNav');
  if(nav&&!nav.dataset.p127Observed){
    nav.dataset.p127Observed='1';
    new MutationObserver(scheduleSync).observe(nav,{childList:true,subtree:true,attributes:true,attributeFilter:['class']});
  }
  const body=document.body;
  if(body&&!body.dataset.p127ViewObserved){
    body.dataset.p127ViewObserved='1';
    new MutationObserver(scheduleSync).observe(body,{attributes:true,attributeFilter:['data-view']});
  }
  const vm=document.getElementById('viewMensagens');
  if(vm&&!vm.dataset.p127Observed){
    vm.dataset.p127Observed='1';
    new MutationObserver(scheduleSync).observe(vm,{childList:true,subtree:false});
  }
  setInterval(syncTabs,1200);
  window.CARBONAUTAS_TABS_BUILD=BUILD;
  console.info('Carbonautas P127 · abas Geral / Panelinha / Privadas sincronizadas');
}

if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',boot,{once:true});else boot();
})();

</script>

<script data-src="tabs-stability-p50.js">
/* Carbonautas P127 · estado correto das abas de conversa.
   Corrige o bug em que "Conversas em panelinha" permanecia verde ao abrir Privadas.
   A aba ativa passa a seguir a tela REAL aberta, e não apenas a existência de DOM escondido. */
(function(){
'use strict';
if(window.__CARBONAUTAS_TABS_P127)return;
window.__CARBONAUTAS_TABS_P127=true;
const BUILD='P127';
let queued=false;

function installStyle(){
  let st=document.getElementById('p50TabsStyle');
  if(!st){st=document.createElement('style');st.id='p50TabsStyle';document.head.appendChild(st)}
  st.textContent=`
    #subNav button{transition:none!important;animation:none!important;}

    /* padrão: qualquer botão não ativo fica branco */
    #subNav [data-sub-view="mensagens"],
    #subNav .p46-panel-btn,
    #subNav [data-sub-view="conversas"]{
      background:#fff!important;
      color:#17313d!important;
      border-color:#cfdde1!important;
      box-shadow:none!important;
      transform:none!important;
    }

    /* somente a classe ON recebe o verde */
    #subNav [data-sub-view="mensagens"].on,
    #subNav .p46-panel-btn.on,
    #subNav [data-sub-view="conversas"].on{
      background:#0d8192!important;
      color:#fff!important;
      border-color:#0d8192!important;
      box-shadow:none!important;
      transform:none!important;
    }

    /* trava de segurança: em Privadas, SOMENTE Privadas pode ficar verde */
    body[data-view="conversas"] #subNav [data-sub-view="mensagens"],
    body[data-view="conversas"] #subNav .p46-panel-btn{
      background:#fff!important;color:#17313d!important;border-color:#cfdde1!important;
    }
    body[data-view="conversas"] #subNav [data-sub-view="conversas"]{
      background:#0d8192!important;color:#fff!important;border-color:#0d8192!important;
    }
  `;
}

function isPanelinhaMounted(){
  const root=document.querySelector('#viewMensagens #p48GroupsRoot');
  if(!root)return false;
  /* Só vale como panelinha ativa quando a própria view de mensagens está ativa. */
  return document.body.dataset.view==='mensagens';
}

function syncTabs(){
  const nav=document.getElementById('subNav');
  if(!nav)return;
  const gen=nav.querySelector('[data-sub-view="mensagens"]');
  const pan=nav.querySelector('.p46-panel-btn');
  const priv=nav.querySelector('[data-sub-view="conversas"]');
  if(!gen&&!pan&&!priv)return;

  const view=document.body.dataset.view||'';
  if(view==='conversas'){
    gen?.classList.remove('on');
    pan?.classList.remove('on');
    priv?.classList.add('on');
    return;
  }
  if(view==='mensagens'){
    priv?.classList.remove('on');
    if(isPanelinhaMounted()){
      gen?.classList.remove('on');
      pan?.classList.add('on');
    }else{
      pan?.classList.remove('on');
      gen?.classList.add('on');
    }
  }
}

function scheduleSync(){
  if(queued)return;queued=true;
  requestAnimationFrame(()=>{queued=false;syncTabs()});
}

function bindClicks(){
  if(document.documentElement.dataset.p127TabsBound==='1')return;
  document.documentElement.dataset.p127TabsBound='1';
  document.addEventListener('click',e=>{
    if(!e.target.closest?.('#subNav button'))return;
    setTimeout(syncTabs,0);
    setTimeout(syncTabs,35);
  },true);
}

function boot(){
  installStyle();bindClicks();syncTabs();
  const nav=document.getElementById('subNav');
  if(nav&&!nav.dataset.p127Observed){
    nav.dataset.p127Observed='1';
    new MutationObserver(scheduleSync).observe(nav,{childList:true,subtree:true,attributes:true,attributeFilter:['class']});
  }
  const body=document.body;
  if(body&&!body.dataset.p127ViewObserved){
    body.dataset.p127ViewObserved='1';
    new MutationObserver(scheduleSync).observe(body,{attributes:true,attributeFilter:['data-view']});
  }
  const vm=document.getElementById('viewMensagens');
  if(vm&&!vm.dataset.p127Observed){
    vm.dataset.p127Observed='1';
    new MutationObserver(scheduleSync).observe(vm,{childList:true,subtree:false});
  }
  setInterval(syncTabs,1200);
  window.CARBONAUTAS_TABS_BUILD=BUILD;
  console.info('Carbonautas P127 · abas Geral / Panelinha / Privadas sincronizadas');
}

if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',boot,{once:true});else boot();
})();

</script>

<script data-src="tabs-stability-p50.js">
/* Carbonautas P127 · estado correto das abas de conversa.
   Corrige o bug em que "Conversas em panelinha" permanecia verde ao abrir Privadas.
   A aba ativa passa a seguir a tela REAL aberta, e não apenas a existência de DOM escondido. */
(function(){
'use strict';
if(window.__CARBONAUTAS_TABS_P127)return;
window.__CARBONAUTAS_TABS_P127=true;
const BUILD='P127';
let queued=false;

function installStyle(){
  let st=document.getElementById('p50TabsStyle');
  if(!st){st=document.createElement('style');st.id='p50TabsStyle';document.head.appendChild(st)}
  st.textContent=`
    #subNav button{transition:none!important;animation:none!important;}

    /* padrão: qualquer botão não ativo fica branco */
    #subNav [data-sub-view="mensagens"],
    #subNav .p46-panel-btn,
    #subNav [data-sub-view="conversas"]{
      background:#fff!important;
      color:#17313d!important;
      border-color:#cfdde1!important;
      box-shadow:none!important;
      transform:none!important;
    }

    /* somente a classe ON recebe o verde */
    #subNav [data-sub-view="mensagens"].on,
    #subNav .p46-panel-btn.on,
    #subNav [data-sub-view="conversas"].on{
      background:#0d8192!important;
      color:#fff!important;
      border-color:#0d8192!important;
      box-shadow:none!important;
      transform:none!important;
    }

    /* trava de segurança: em Privadas, SOMENTE Privadas pode ficar verde */
    body[data-view="conversas"] #subNav [data-sub-view="mensagens"],
    body[data-view="conversas"] #subNav .p46-panel-btn{
      background:#fff!important;color:#17313d!important;border-color:#cfdde1!important;
    }
    body[data-view="conversas"] #subNav [data-sub-view="conversas"]{
      background:#0d8192!important;color:#fff!important;border-color:#0d8192!important;
    }
  `;
}

function isPanelinhaMounted(){
  const root=document.querySelector('#viewMensagens #p48GroupsRoot');
  if(!root)return false;
  /* Só vale como panelinha ativa quando a própria view de mensagens está ativa. */
  return document.body.dataset.view==='mensagens';
}

function syncTabs(){
  const nav=document.getElementById('subNav');
  if(!nav)return;
  const gen=nav.querySelector('[data-sub-view="mensagens"]');
  const pan=nav.querySelector('.p46-panel-btn');
  const priv=nav.querySelector('[data-sub-view="conversas"]');
  if(!gen&&!pan&&!priv)return;

  const view=document.body.dataset.view||'';
  if(view==='conversas'){
    gen?.classList.remove('on');
    pan?.classList.remove('on');
    priv?.classList.add('on');
    return;
  }
  if(view==='mensagens'){
    priv?.classList.remove('on');
    if(isPanelinhaMounted()){
      gen?.classList.remove('on');
      pan?.classList.add('on');
    }else{
      pan?.classList.remove('on');
      gen?.classList.add('on');
    }
  }
}

function scheduleSync(){
  if(queued)return;queued=true;
  requestAnimationFrame(()=>{queued=false;syncTabs()});
}

function bindClicks(){
  if(document.documentElement.dataset.p127TabsBound==='1')return;
  document.documentElement.dataset.p127TabsBound='1';
  document.addEventListener('click',e=>{
    if(!e.target.closest?.('#subNav button'))return;
    setTimeout(syncTabs,0);
    setTimeout(syncTabs,35);
  },true);
}

function boot(){
  installStyle();bindClicks();syncTabs();
  const nav=document.getElementById('subNav');
  if(nav&&!nav.dataset.p127Observed){
    nav.dataset.p127Observed='1';
    new MutationObserver(scheduleSync).observe(nav,{childList:true,subtree:true,attributes:true,attributeFilter:['class']});
  }
  const body=document.body;
  if(body&&!body.dataset.p127ViewObserved){
    body.dataset.p127ViewObserved='1';
    new MutationObserver(scheduleSync).observe(body,{attributes:true,attributeFilter:['data-view']});
  }
  const vm=document.getElementById('viewMensagens');
  if(vm&&!vm.dataset.p127Observed){
    vm.dataset.p127Observed='1';
    new MutationObserver(scheduleSync).observe(vm,{childList:true,subtree:false});
  }
  setInterval(syncTabs,1200);
  window.CARBONAUTAS_TABS_BUILD=BUILD;
  console.info('Carbonautas P127 · abas Geral / Panelinha / Privadas sincronizadas');
}

if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',boot,{once:true});else boot();
})();

</script>

function personById(id){try{return typeof memberById==='function'?memberById(id):null}catch(e){return null}}
function versionCount(p){
  try{
    const tid=p?.reviewThreadId||p?.id;
    if(typeof reviewThreadItems==='function')return reviewThreadItems(tid).length;
    if(typeof state!=='undefined'&&Array.isArray(state.publicacoes))return state.publicacoes.filter(x=>x.reviewFlow&&(x.reviewThreadId||x.id)===tid).length;
  }catch(e){}
  return Number(p?.reviewVersion||1)||1;
}
function nameOf(p,id,fallback=''){const m=personById(id);return m?.nome||fallback||''}
function flowHTML(p){
  if(!p?.reviewFlow)return '';
  const v=Number(p.reviewVersion||1)||1;
  const author=nameOf(p,p.memberId,p.memberNome||'Autor do arquivo')||'Autor do arquivo';
  const reviewer=nameOf(p,p.reviewReviewerId,'Revisor')||'Revisor';
  const next=nameOf(p,p.reviewNextRecipientId,'');
  const vc=versionCount(p);
  let now='',last='',tone='review';
  if(p.reviewStatus==='correcao_devolvida'){
    tone='adjust';
    now=next?`${next} está com a versão ${v} para ajustar`:`A versão ${v} voltou para ajustes`;
    last=`${reviewer} devolveu a correção`;
  }else if(p.reviewStatus==='nova_versao'){
    tone='review';
    now=next?`${next} está com a versão ${v} para revisar`:`Versão ${v} enviada para revisão`;
    last=`${author} enviou a versão ${v}`;
  }else{
    tone='review';
    now=next?`${next} está com a versão ${v} para revisar`:`Aguardando revisão da versão ${v}`;
    last=`${author} enviou o arquivo para correção`;
  }
  const reviewerExtra=reviewer&&next&&reviewer!==next?`<span>👤 Revisor responsável: <b>${esc52(reviewer)}</b></span>`:'';
  return `<div class="p52-review-flow ${tone}">
    <div class="p52-now"><span class="p52-label">AGORA</span><b>${esc52(now)}</b></div>
    <div class="p52-last"><span>↳ Última ação: <b>${esc52(last)}</b></span><span>🗂 ${vc} ${vc===1?'versão':'versões'} no histórico</span>${reviewerExtra}</div>
  </div>`;
}
function injectCss(){
  if(document.getElementById('p52ReviewStyle'))return;
  const st=document.createElement('style');st.id='p52ReviewStyle';st.textContent=`
  .p52-review-flow{margin:8px 0 5px;

--- occurrence 22 at char 872695 ---
ew="mensagens"],
    body[data-view="conversas"] #subNav .p46-panel-btn{
      background:#fff!important;color:#17313d!important;border-color:#cfdde1!important;
    }
    body[data-view="conversas"] #subNav [data-sub-view="conversas"]{
      background:#0d8192!important;color:#fff!important;border-color:#0d8192!important;
    }
  `;
}

function isPanelinhaMounted(){
  const root=document.querySelector('#viewMensagens #p48GroupsRoot');
  if(!root)return false;
  /* Só vale como panelinha ativa quando a própria view de mensagens está ativa. */
  return document.body.dataset.view==='mensagens';
}

function syncTabs(){
  const nav=document.getElementById('subNav');
  if(!nav)return;
  const gen=nav.querySelector('[data-sub-view="mensagens"]');
  const pan=nav.querySelector('.p46-panel-btn');
  const priv=nav.querySelector('[data-sub-view="conversas"]');
  if(!gen&&!pan&&!priv)return;

  const view=document.body.dataset.view||'';
  if(view==='conversas'){
    gen?.classList.remove('on');
    pan?.classList.remove('on');
    priv?.classList.add('on');
    return;
  }
  if(view==='mensagens'){
    priv?.classList.remove('on');
    if(isPanelinhaMounted()){
      gen?.classList.remove('on');
      pan?.classList.add('on');
    }else{
      pan?.classList.remove('on');
      gen?.classList.add('on');
    }
  }
}

function scheduleSync(){
  if(queued)return;queued=true;
  requestAnimationFrame(()=>{queued=false;syncTabs()});
}

function bindClicks(){
  if(document.documentElement.dataset.p127TabsBound==='1')return;
  document.documentElement.dataset.p127TabsBound='1';
  document.addEventListener('click',e=>{
    if(!e.target.closest?.('#subNav button'))return;
    setTimeout(syncTabs,0);
    setTimeout(syncTabs,35);
  },true);
}

function boot(){
  installStyle();bindClicks();syncTabs();
  const nav=document.getElementById('subNav');
  if(nav&&!nav.dataset.p127Observed){
    nav.dataset.p127Observed='1';
    new MutationObserver(scheduleSync).observe(nav,{childList:true,subtree:true,attributes:true,attributeFilter:['class']});
  }
  const body=document.body;
  if(body&&!body.dataset.p127ViewObserved){
    body.dataset.p127ViewObserved='1';
    new MutationObserver(scheduleSync).observe(body,{attributes:true,attributeFilter:['data-view']});
  }
  const vm=document.getElementById('viewMensagens');
  if(vm&&!vm.dataset.p127Observed){
    vm.dataset.p127Observed='1';
    new MutationObserver(scheduleSync).observe(vm,{childList:true,subtree:false});
  }
  setInterval(syncTabs,1200);
  window.CARBONAUTAS_TABS_BUILD=BUILD;
  console.info('Carbonautas P127 · abas Geral / Panelinha / Privadas sincronizadas');
}

if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',boot,{once:true});else boot();
})();

</script>
<script data-src="agenda-badge-p51.js">
/* Carbonautas P51 · remove contador confuso da aba Agenda */
(function(){
'use strict';
function apply(){
  document.querySelectorAll('#cronoBadge').forEach(b=>{
    b.textContent='';
    b.style.setProperty('display','none','important');
    b.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');
  });
}
function inject(){
  if(document.getElementById('p51AgendaBadgeStyle'))return;
  const st=document.createElement('style');
  st.id='p51AgendaBadgeStyle';
  st.textContent='#cronoBadge{display:none!important}';
  document.head.appendChild(st);
}
function loadP116(){
  if(document.getElementById('carbonautasP116Script'))return;
  const s=document.createElement('script');
  s.id='carbonautasP116Script';
  s.src='./chat-notifications-reactions-p116.js?v=P116-20260918';
  s.async=false;
  document.head.appendChild(s);
}
function boot(){inject();apply();loadP116();setTimeout(apply,300);setTimeout(apply,1200);console.info('Carbonautas agenda badge P51 + P116 carregado')}
if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',boot,{once:true});else boot();
})();

<script data-src="group-chat-notifications-p80.js">
/* Carbonautas P80 · notificações de Panelinhas sem transformar Fofoca geral em spam */
(function(){
'use strict';
const VERSION='P80';
let myMemberId='',me={},initialized=false,notifMap=new Map(),unsubChat=null,unsubNotif=null;
const $=(s,r=document)=>r.querySelector(s), $$=(s,r=document)=>[...r.querySelectorAll(s)];
function F(){return window.fbFns}
function sleep(ms){return new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r,ms))}
async function identity(){const f=F(),uid=window.auth?.currentUser?.uid;if(!f||!uid)return false;try{const u=await f.getDoc(f.doc(window.db,'rede_users',uid));if(!u.exists())return false;me=u.data();myMemberId=me.memberId||'';return !!myMemberId}catch(e){console.warn('P80 identidade',e);return false}}
function stampId(v=''){return String(v).replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9_-]/g,'_').slice(0,160)}
async function notifyGroupMessage(msg,docs){if(!msg?.groupId||msg.authorId!==myMemberId)return;const meta=docs.find(x=>x.tipo==='chat_group_meta'&&x.groupId===msg.groupId&&x.active!==false);if(!meta)return;const ids=[...new Set((meta.memberIds||[]).filter(x=>x&&x!==myMemberId))];if(!ids.length)return;const f=F(),batch=f.writeBatch(window.db),uid=window.auth.currentUser.uid,title=`👥 ${meta.groupName||'Panelinha'} · nova mensagem`,message=msg.imageUrl?'📷 Nova foto/print no grupo.':String(msg.text||'Nova mensagem no grupo.').slice(0,240);ids.forEach(recipientId=>{const id=`grp_${stampId(msg.id)}_${stampId(recipientId)}`,ref=f.doc(window.db,'rede_notifications',id);batch.set(ref,{recipientId,senderId:myMemberId,senderUid:uid,senderName:me.nome||'Carbonauta',kind:'group_message',title,message,sourceType:'group_chat',sourceId:msg.groupId,read:false,ts:f.serverTimestamp()})});try{await batch.commit()}catch(e){if(e?.code!=='permission-denied')console.warn('P80 notificação de grupo',e)}}
async function subscribeChat(){if(unsubChat)return;const f=F();const q=f.query(f.collection(window.db,'rede_chat'),f.orderBy('ts','asc'));unsubChat=f.onSnapshot(q,snap=>{const docs=snap.docs.map(d=>({...d.data(),id:d.id}));if(initialized){snap.docChanges().filter(ch=>ch.type==='added').forEach(ch=>{const m={...ch.doc.data(),id:ch.doc.id};if(m.tipo==='chat_group_message'&&m.authorId===myMemberId)notifyGroupMessage(m,docs)})}initialized=true},e=>console.warn('P80 chat',e))}
async function subscribeNotifs(){if(unsubNotif||!myMemberId)return;const f=F(),q=f.query(f.collection(window.db,'rede_notifications'),f.where('recipientId','==',myMemberId));unsubNotif=f.onSnapshot(q,snap=>{notifMap=new Map(snap.docs.map(d=>[d.id,{...d.data(),id:d.id}]));setTimeout(decorate,20)},e=>console.warn('P80 notificações',e))}
function decorate(){$$('[data-p78-notif]').forEach(b=>{const n=notifMap.get(b.dataset.p78Notif);if(n?.sourceType!=='group_chat')return;const tag=$('.p78-tag',b);if(tag){tag.textContent='GRUPO';tag.classList.add('group')}});$$('.notify-item[data-nid]').forEach(b=>{const n=notifMap.get(b.dataset.nid);if(n?.sourceType==='group_chat')b.dataset.p80Group='1'})}
async function openGroupNotification(n){if(!n)return;const f=F();try{await f.updateDoc(f.doc(window.db,'rede_notifications',n.id),{read:true,readAt:f.serverTimestamp()})}catch(_e){};try{document.querySelectorAll('.overlay.open').forEach(o=>o.classList.remove('open'))}catch(_e){};const tab=document.querySelector('.tab[data-view="mensagens"],[data-view="mensagens"]');if(tab)tab.click();else if(typeof window.switchView==='function')window.switchView('mensagens');await sleep(250);let btn=document.querySelector(`[data-p46-group="${CSS.escape(n.sourceId||'')}"]`);if(!btn){await sleep(450);btn=document.querySelector(`[data-p46-group="${CSS.escape(n.sourceId||'')}"]`)}if(btn)btn.click()}
document.addEventListener('click',e=>{const p=e.target.closest('[data-p78-notif]');if(p){const n=notifMap.get(p.dataset.p78Notif);if(n?.sourceType==='group_chat'){e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();e.stopImmediatePropagation();openGroupNotification(n);return}}const b=e.target.closest('.notify-item[data-nid]');if(b){const n=notifMap.get(b.dataset.nid);if(n?.sourceType==='group_chat'){e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();e.stopImmediatePropagation();openGroupNotification(n)}}},true);
async function boot(){let tries=0;while(tries++<80){if(window.fbFns&&window.db&&window.auth?.currentUser)break;await sleep(250)}if(!await identity())return;await subscribeChat();await subscribeNotifs();setInterval(decorate,800);console.info('Carbonautas',VERSION,'notificações de panelinhas carregadas')}
if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',boot,{once:true});else boot();
})();
