  if(purgeStarted)return;purgeStarted=true;
  try{await deleteIds(notifications().filter(n=>n.read).map(n=>n.id))}catch(e){console.warn('P51 purge read',e)}
}
function ensurePanel(){
  let p=document.getElementById('notifyPanelP39');
  if(p)return p;
  p=document.createElement('div');p.id='notifyPanelP39';
  p.style.cssText='position:fixed;inset:0;z-index:2147483646;background:rgba(3,16,27,.72);display:none;align-items:flex-start;justify-content:center;padding:18px 12px;overflow:auto;-webkit-overflow-scrolling:touch';
  p.innerHTML=`<div id="notifyCardP39" style="width:min(640px,100%);max-height:calc(100dvh - 36px);overflow:hidden;background:#fff;border-radius:20px;box-shadow:0 18px 70px rgba(0,0,0,.38);display:flex;flex-direction:column;margin:auto">
    <div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:12px;padding:18px 18px 14px;border-bottom:1px solid #dbe5ea;background:#f4f8fb">
      <div style="font-size:25px">🔔</div><div style="flex:1;min-width:0"><div style="font:800 20px/1.1 system-ui;color:#071827">Notificações</div><div id="notifySubP39" style="font:500 12px/1.4 system-ui;color:#6b7f89;margin-top:3px"></div></div>
      <button id="notifyCloseP39" type="button" style="border:0;background:#e9eff3;border-radius:12px;width:42px;height:42px;font-size:24px;color:#142833">×</button>
    </div>
    <div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;flex-wrap:wrap;padding:12px 16px;border-bottom:1px solid #edf1f3">
      <button id="notifyReadP39" type="button" style="border:1px solid #cbd8df;background:#fff;border-radius:10px;padding:9px 12px;font:700 12px system-ui;color:#17313d">🧹 Limpar todas</button>
      <span style="font:500 11px/1.35 system-ui;color:#73858d">Ao abrir uma notificação, ela sai desta lista.</span>
    </div>
    <div id="notifyListP39" style="padding:12px;overflow:auto;-webkit-overflow-scrolling:touch;min-height:120px;max-height:68dvh"></div>
  </div>`;
  document.body.appendChild(p);
  p.addEventListener('click',e=>{if(e.target===p)closePanel()});
  p.querySelector('#notifyCloseP39').onclick=closePanel;
  p.querySelector('#notifyReadP39').onclick=async()=>{
    const arr=notifications();
    if(!arr.length)return;
    if(!confirm(`Limpar ${arr.length} notificação${arr.length===1?'':'ões'}?`))return;
    try{await deleteIds(arr.map(n=>n.id))}catch(e){console.warn('P51 clear all',e)}
    setTimeout(renderPanel,250);
  };
  return p;
}
function renderPanel(){
  const p=ensurePanel(),list=p.querySelector('#notifyListP39'),sub=p.querySelector('#notifySubP39'),arr=unreadNotifications();
  sub.textContent=arr.length?`${arr.length} ${arr.length===1?'aviso pendente':'avisos pendentes'}`:'Tudo visto ✓';
  if(!arr.length){list.innerHTML='<div style="padding:42px 18px;text-align:center;color:#71838b;font:600 14px system-ui"><div style="font-size:34px;margin-bottom:8px">✓</div>Nenhuma notificação pendente.</div>';return}
  list.innerHTML=arr.slice(0,100).map(n=>`<button type="button" data-p39nid="${esc2(n.id)}" style="width:100%;text-align:left;border:1px solid #bcdde8;background:#f7fcfe;border-radius:14px;padding:12px;margin:0 0 9px;display:flex;gap:11px;align-items:flex-start;color:#132a35"><span style="font-size:22px;line-height:1">${icon(n)}</span><span style="display:block;min-width:0;flex:1"><b style="display:block;font:800 13px/1.35 system-ui;color:#10283a">${esc2(n.title||'Nova notificação')}</b><span style="display:block;font:500 12.5px/1.45 system-ui;color:#4a5c62;margin-top:3px">${esc2(n.message||'')}</span><small style="display:block;font:500 10.5px/1.35 system-ui;color:#7c8b90;margin-top:5px">${esc2(n.senderName||'Carbonauta')} · ${esc2(timeText(n))}</small></span></button>`).join('');
  list.querySelectorAll('[data-p39nid]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=async()=>{
    const id=b.getAttribute('data-p39nid');
    closePanel();
    try{
      if(typeof openNotification==='function')await openNotification(id);
      else{
        const n=notifications().find(x=>x.id===id),f=fns();
        if(n&&!n.read&&f)await f.updateDoc(f.doc(window.db,'rede_notifications',id),{read:true,readAt:f.serverTimestamp()});
      }
    }catch(e){console.warn('P51 openNotification',e)}
    try{await deleteIds([id])}catch(e){console.warn('P51 remove viewed notification',e)}
  });
}
function openPanel(){
  const now=Date.now();if(now-lastOpen<180)return;lastOpen=now;
  const old=document.getElementById('notifyOverlay');if(old){old.classList.remove('open');old.style.display='none'}
  const p=ensurePanel();renderPanel();p.style.display='flex';document.documentElement.style.overflow='hidden';
  purgeRead();
}
function closePanel(){const p=document.getElementById('notifyPanelP39');if(p)p.style.display='none';document.documentElement.style.overflow=''}
function bind(){
  const btn=document.getElementById('notifyBtn');if(btn){btn.style.pointerEvents='auto';btn.style.position='relative';btn.style.zIndex='2147483000';btn.title='Notificações';}
  const badge=document.getElementById('notifyBadge');if(badge)badge.style.pointerEvents='none';
}
function intercept(e){const t=e.target?.closest?.('#notifyBtn');if(!t)return;e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();if(e.stopImmediatePropagation)e.stopImmediatePropagation();openPanel()}
document.addEventListener('pointerdown',intercept,true);
document.addEventListener('click',intercept,true);
if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',bind,{once:true});else bind();
new MutationObserver(bind).observe(document.documentElement,{subtree:true,childList:true});
setInterval(()=>{const p=document.getElementById('notifyPanelP39');if(p&&p.style.display==='flex')renderPanel()},1200);
window.openNotificationsP39=openPanel;
console.info('Carbonautas notifications',VERSION,'carregado');
})();

</script>

function uniqueCount(points,action){return new Set((points||[]).filter(p=>p.action===action).map(p=>String(p.sourceId||p.id||Math.random()))).size}
function correctionDoneCount(points,memberId){const ids=new Set((points||[]).filter(p=>p.memberId===memberId&&p.action==='activity_completed').map(p=>String(p.sourceId||'')));let n=0;ids.forEach(id=>{const a=(S()?.activities||[]).find(x=>String(x.id)===id);if(a?.type==='correcao')n++});return n}

function metric(memberObj,points,mk){
  const mine=(points||[]).filter(p=>p.memberId===memberObj.id),completed=uniqueCount(mine,'activity_completed'),deadline=uniqueCount(mine,'deadline_done'),pubs=uniqueCount(mine,'publication'),products=uniqueCount(mine,'product'),help=uniqueCount(mine,'help_post'),check=uniqueCount(mine,'checkin'),packages=uniqueCount(mine,'repo_package'),corrections=correctionDoneCount(mine,memberObj.id);const delivery=Math.min(40,Math.min(24,completed*8)+Math.min(16,deadline*8)),production=Math.min(30,Math.min(20,pubs*10)+Math.min(10,products*5)),collaboration=Math.min(20,Math.min(16,help*8)+Math.min(8,corrections*4)),organization=Math.min(10,Math.min(5,check*5)+Math.min(2,packages*2)+Math.min(3,deadline)),bonus=bonusFor(memberObj.id,mk),base=delivery+production+collaboration+organization,score=Math.min(100,base+bonus),critical=mk===currentMonthKey()?isOverdueFor(memberObj.id):0;let medal='Em progresso',icon='🌱',next=50;if(score>=95&&critical===0){medal='Carbonauta Topizera';icon='💎';next=100}else if(score>=85){medal='Ouro';icon='🥇';next=95}else if(score>=70){medal='Prata';icon='🥈';next=85}else if(score>=50){medal='Bronze';icon='🥉';next=70}return {memberId:memberObj.id,name:memberObj.nome||'Carbonauta',score,delivery,production,collaboration,organization,bonus,critical,medal,icon,next,events:mine.length+achievementCount(memberObj.id,mk)}
}

function rowHtml(highlightId){
  const own=mine(highlightId)?.reaction||'';
  return Object.entries(TYPES).map(([key,t])=>{
    const n=count(highlightId,key),on=own===key;
    return `<button type="button" class="p123-react${on?' on':''}" data-r="${key}" data-highlight-id="${highlightId}" aria-pressed="${on?'true':'false'}" title="${t.label}"><span class="em">${t.emoji}</span><span>${t.label}</span><span class="n">${n||''}</span></button>`;
  }).join('');
}

    createError=e;
    // Se a conversa já existia, o setDoc é tratado como UPDATE e pode ser
    // recusado (corretamente). Nesse caso, lemos a thread existente.
    try{
      const snap=await f.getDoc(ref);
      if(snap.exists())threadData={...snap.data(),id};
    }catch(readError){
      console.warn('Não foi possível ler uma thread existente:',readError);
    }
  }
  if(!threadData){
    console.error('Falha ao criar/abrir conversa privada:',createError);
    if(createError?.code==='permission-denied')toast('Permissão negada ao iniciar a conversa. Recarregue o app; se persistir, confira o vínculo desta conta com a Rede.');
    else toast('Não consegui abrir a conversa privada.');
    return;
  }
  if(!state.privateThreads.some(t=>t.id===id))state.privateThreads=[threadData,...state.privateThreads];
  closeOverlay('privateNewOverlay');switchView('conversas');openPrivateThread(id);
}

async function createGroup(){
  const name=norm($('#p48Name')?.value);
  if(!name)return toast('Dê um nome para a panelinha.');

  const uniq=[...new Set(ids)];

    const localThread={id:ref.id,groupName,memberIds:uniq,createdByUid:window.auth.currentUser.uid,createdByMemberId:myMemberId,createdByName,active:true,lastMessage:'',createdAt:{toMillis:()=>Date.now()},updatedAt:{toMillis:()=>Date.now()}};
    threads=[localThread,...threads.filter(x=>x.id!==ref.id)];
    $('#p48GroupOverlay').style.display='none';
    activeThreadId=ref.id;
    mount();renderList();renderHead();renderMessages();subscribeMessages(ref.id);
    $('#p48Shell')?.classList.add('chat-open');

  }catch(e){
    console.error('P48 create group',e);showRulesWarning();

  }finally{
    if(btn){btn.disabled=false;btn.textContent='Criar panelinha'}
  }
}

      notifInitial=false;
    },err=>console.warn('Notificações não disponíveis:',err)));
  }

  // DESLIGAMENTO EM TEMPO REAL — integrantes egressos perdem a sessão imediatamente.
  if(myId && !isAdmin){
    subscriptions.push(f.onSnapshot(f.doc(window.db,'rede_members',myId),snap=>{
      if(!snap.exists()||snap.data()?.status==='egresso'){
        doLogout().finally(()=>showLogin('Seu vínculo com esta rede foi encerrado. O histórico acadêmico permanece preservado.'));
      }
    },err=>console.warn('Monitoramento do vínculo:',err)));
  }

  // CONTAS (somente coordenador pode listar todas)
  if(isAdmin){
    subscriptions.push(f.onSnapshot(f.collection(window.db,'rede_users'),snap=>{
      state.users=snap.docs.map(d=>({...d.data(),uid:d.id}));
      renderAccessList();
      if(!privateDirectorySeeded){privateDirectorySeeded=true;seedPrivateDirectoryFromUsers().catch(e=>console.warn('Não foi possível preparar o diretório privado:',e));}
    }));
  } else { state.users=authProfile?[{...authProfile,uid:authUser.uid}]:[]; }
}

async function fbUploadImage(pathPrefix,dataUrl){
  const f=FB();
  const au=window.auth.currentUser; if(!au)throw new Error('Sessão encerrada.');
  const path=`rede_uploads/${au.uid}/${pathPrefix}_${Date.now()}_${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2,7)}.jpg`;
  const r=f.sRef(window.storage,path);
  await f.uploadString(r,dataUrl,'data_url');
  const url=await f.getDownloadURL(r);
  return {url,path};
}

}

async function startPrivateConversation(targetMemberId){
  if(!targetMemberId||targetMemberId===myId)return;
  const target=await resolvePrivateTarget(targetMemberId);
  if(!target?.uid){toast('Essa pessoa ainda não está disponível para chat privado.');return}
  const uidv=privateUid(),pairs=[{memberId:myId,uid:uidv},{memberId:targetMemberId,uid:target.uid}].sort((a,b)=>a.memberId.localeCompare(b.memberId)),id=privateThreadId(myId,targetMemberId),f=FB(),ref=f.doc(window.db,'rede_private_threads',id);

  // P10: não fazemos GET antes do CREATE. Em regras privadas, um GET de um
  // documento que ainda não existe não tem resource.data e pode retornar
  // permission-denied mesmo quando as regras corretas já estão publicadas.
  const local=state.privateThreads.find(t=>t.id===id);
  if(local){closeOverlay('privateNewOverlay');switchView('conversas');openPrivateThread(id);return}

  const unread={};pairs.forEach(x=>unread[x.uid]=0);
  const baseThread={participants:pairs.map(x=>x.uid),memberIds:pairs.map(x=>x.memberId),createdAt:new Date(),updatedAt:new Date(),lastMessage:'',lastSenderUid:'',lastSenderMemberId:'',unreadBy:unread};
  let threadData=null,createError=null;
  try{
    await f.setDoc(ref,{...baseThread,createdAt:f.serverTimestamp(),updatedAt:f.serverTimestamp()});
    threadData={...baseThread,id};
  }catch(e){
    createError=e;
    // Se a conversa já existia, o setDoc é tratado como UPDATE e pode ser
    // recusado (corretamente). Nesse caso, lemos a thread existente.
    try{
      const snap=await f.getDoc(ref);
      if(snap.exists())threadData={...snap.data(),id};
    }catch(readError){
      console.warn('Não foi possível ler uma thread existente:',readError);
    }
  }
  if(!threadData){
    console.error('Falha ao criar/abrir conversa privada:',createError);
    if(createError?.code==='permission-denied')toast('Permissão negada ao iniciar a conversa. Recarregue o app; se persistir, confira o vínculo desta conta com a Rede.');
    else toast('Não consegui abrir a conversa privada.');
    return;
  }
  if(!state.privateThreads.some(t=>t.id===id))state.privateThreads=[threadData,...state.privateThreads];
  closeOverlay('privateNewOverlay');switchView('conversas');openPrivateThread(id);
}

function normalizeContext(ctx={}){
  const type=['file','correction','orientation','deadline'].includes(ctx.type)?ctx.type:'file',icon=ctx.icon||(type==='correction'?'✍️':type==='orientation'?'🧭':type==='deadline'?'⏰':'📎'),label=ctx.label||(type==='correction'?'CORREÇÃO':type==='orientation'?'ORIENTAÇÃO':type==='deadline'?'PRAZO':'ARQUIVO');
  return {type,icon,label,title:String(ctx.title||ctx.subject||'Referência').slice(0,220),version:String(ctx.version||'').slice(0,40),eventLabel:String(ctx.eventLabel||ctx.subtitle||'').slice(0,180),sourceId:String(ctx.sourceId||'').slice(0,180)};
}
function compactContext(ctx){const c=normalizeContext(ctx),parts=[`${c.icon} ${c.label}`,c.title];if(c.version)parts.push(c.version);if(c.eventLabel)parts.push(c.eventLabel);return parts.filter(Boolean).join(' · ').slice(0,158)}

  const parts=t.split(' · ').map(x=>x.trim()).filter(Boolean);return {type,icon,label,title:parts[1]||'Referência',meta:parts.slice(2).join(' · ')};
}
